## Purpose
This report consolidates the SQL exploration and description-level analysis used to understand the current OpenAI role population before choosing Application #001. It is deliberately transparent: the structured fields, secondary locations, keyword signals, compensation intervals, and data-quality limitations are all shown separately.
## Interpretive guardrails
- present_in_api=1 is treated as the current role population.
- is_remote=1 is NOT treated as equivalent to workplace_type=Remote.
- Primary and secondary locations are analyzed separately.
- US - Remote as a secondary location is evidence of possible geographic flexibility, not proof that Utah is permitted.
- Description keyword overlap is a discovery aid, not a qualification or fit score.
- Sales signals identify roles needing exclusion/review; absence of a signal does not prove a role is non-sales.
- Experience-year detection captures mentions, not necessarily hard requirements.
- Education detection captures mentions, not necessarily hard requirements.
- Compensation values must be interpreted together with their interval and currency.
- No role is selected or rejected solely by this battery.

## 15. Experience-year language
The parser recognizes more patterns than the original SQL CASE statement, including `5+ years`, `at least 5 years`, ranges, and spelled-out forms such as `five years of`. These are still mentions, not automatically hard requirements.

## 19. SQL catalog
These are the exact SQL statements executed by this battery. They are included so every table in the report can be reproduced manually against the same database.

### source_status_counts

```sql
SELECT source_status, COUNT(*) AS roles
            FROM roles
            GROUP BY source_status
            ORDER BY source_status
```

### population_summary

```sql
SELECT
                COUNT(*) AS current_roles,
                SUM(CASE WHEN present_in_api=1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)
                    AS api_current,
                SUM(CASE WHEN present_in_html=1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)
                    AS html_present
            FROM roles
```

### work_arrangement_distribution

```sql
SELECT
                COALESCE(CAST(is_remote AS TEXT), 'NULL') AS is_remote,
                COALESCE(workplace_type, 'NULL') AS workplace_type,
                COUNT(*) AS roles
            FROM roles
            WHERE present_in_api = 1
            GROUP BY is_remote, workplace_type
            ORDER BY roles DESC
```

### primary_location_distribution

```sql
SELECT
                COALESCE(display_location, 'NULL') AS location,
                COUNT(*) AS roles
            FROM roles
            WHERE present_in_api = 1
            GROUP BY display_location
            ORDER BY roles DESC, display_location
```

### team_distribution

```sql
SELECT team, COUNT(*) AS roles
            FROM roles
            WHERE present_in_api = 1
            GROUP BY team
            ORDER BY roles DESC, team
```

### broad_structured_remote_pool

```sql
SELECT
                ashby_id,
                title,
                team,
                display_location,
                COALESCE(CAST(is_remote AS TEXT), 'NULL') AS is_remote,
                COALESCE(workplace_type, 'NULL') AS workplace_type
            FROM roles
            WHERE present_in_api = 1
              AND (
                    is_remote = 1
                    OR LOWER(COALESCE(workplace_type,'')) = 'remote'
                  )
            ORDER BY team, title
```

### true_remote_roles

```sql
SELECT
                ashby_id,
                title,
                team,
                display_location,
                workplace_type,
                is_remote
            FROM roles
            WHERE present_in_api = 1
              AND workplace_type = 'Remote'
            ORDER BY team, title
```

### remote_location_conflicts

```sql
SELECT
                ashby_id,
                title,
                team,
                display_location,
                workplace_type,
                is_remote
            FROM roles
            WHERE present_in_api = 1
              AND (
                    (
                        display_location = 'US - Remote'
                        AND COALESCE(workplace_type,'') <> 'Remote'
                    )
                    OR
                    (
                        display_location NOT LIKE '%Remote%'
                        AND workplace_type = 'Remote'
                    )
                  )
            ORDER BY display_location, team, title
```

### us_remote_anywhere

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT
                r.ashby_id,
                r.title,
                r.team,
                r.display_location AS primary_location,
                COALESCE(r.workplace_type,'NULL') AS workplace_type,
                COALESCE(CAST(r.is_remote AS TEXT),'NULL') AS is_remote,
                CASE
                    WHEN r.display_location='US - Remote'
                    THEN 'PRIMARY'
                    WHEN EXISTS (
                        SELECT 1
                        FROM api_secondary_locations s
                        WHERE s.ashby_id=r.ashby_id
                          AND s.location='US - Remote'
                    )
                    THEN 'SECONDARY'
                    ELSE 'NONE'
                END AS us_remote_source
            FROM roles r
            WHERE r.present_in_api=1
              AND (
                    r.display_location='US - Remote'
                    OR EXISTS (
                        SELECT 1
                        FROM api_secondary_locations s
                        WHERE s.ashby_id=r.ashby_id
                          AND s.location='US - Remote'
                    )
                  )
            ORDER BY r.team, r.title
```

### secondary_locations

```sql
SELECT
                r.ashby_id,
                r.title,
                r.team,
                r.display_location AS primary_location,
                s.location AS secondary_location,
                COALESCE(r.workplace_type,'NULL') AS workplace_type
            FROM roles r
            JOIN api_secondary_locations s
              ON s.ashby_id=r.ashby_id
            WHERE r.present_in_api=1
            ORDER BY r.team, r.title, s.location_index
```

### relevant_family_feature_map

```sql
SELECT
                title,
                team,
                display_location,
                COALESCE(workplace_type,'NULL') AS workplace_type,
                CASE
                    WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE
                         '%relocation assistance%'
                    THEN 'YES' ELSE 'NO'
                END AS relocation_assistance_phrase,
                CASE
                    WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE '%remote%'
                    THEN 'YES' ELSE 'NO'
                END AS mentions_remote,
                CASE
                    WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE '%api%'
                    THEN 'YES' ELSE 'NO'
                END AS api,
                CASE
                    WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE
                         '%troubleshoot%'
                    THEN 'YES' ELSE 'NO'
                END AS troubleshooting,
                CASE
                    WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE '%automat%'
                    THEN 'YES' ELSE 'NO'
                END AS automation,
                CASE
                    WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE '%python%'
                      OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE
                         '%javascript%'
                      OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE '%script%'
                    THEN 'YES' ELSE 'NO'
                END AS scripting
            FROM roles
            WHERE present_in_api=1
              AND team IN (
                    'Support Delivery',
                    'Support Operations',
                    'Support Automation',
                    'IT',
                    'Security',
                    'Trust & Safety',
                    'Intelligence & Investigations',
                    'Technical Success',
                    'User Operations',
                    'Technical Program Management'
                  )
            ORDER BY team, title
```

### resume_keyword_overlap

```sql
WITH signals AS (
            SELECT
                ashby_id,
                title,
                team,
                display_location,
                workplace_type,
                is_remote,
                CASE WHEN (LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ?) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS support,
            CASE WHEN (LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ?) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS troubleshooting,
            CASE WHEN (LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ?) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS api_integrations,
            CASE WHEN (LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ?) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS automation,
            CASE WHEN (LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ?) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS scripting_programming,
            CASE WHEN (LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ?) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS sql_data,
            CASE WHEN (LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ?) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS ticketing_itsm,
            CASE WHEN (LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ?) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS network_dns,
            CASE WHEN (LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ?) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS uat_testing,
            CASE WHEN (LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ?) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS documentation,
            CASE WHEN (LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? OR LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ?) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS crm_saas
            FROM roles
            WHERE present_in_api = 1
        )
        SELECT
            ashby_id,
            title,
            team,
            display_location,
            workplace_type,
            is_remote,
            support, troubleshooting, api_integrations, automation, scripting_programming, sql_data, ticketing_itsm, network_dns, uat_testing, documentation, crm_saas,
            (support + troubleshooting + api_integrations + automation + scripting_programming + sql_data + ticketing_itsm + network_dns + uat_testing + documentation + crm_saas) AS resume_keyword_overlap
        FROM signals
        ORDER BY resume_keyword_overlap DESC, team, title
```

### sales_signal_roles

```sql
WITH sales_signals AS (
            SELECT
                ashby_id,
                title,
                team,
                display_location,
                workplace_type,
                CASE WHEN team = 'Sales' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS sales_team,
                CASE
                    WHEN LOWER(title) LIKE '%sales%'
                      OR LOWER(title) LIKE '%account director%'
                      OR LOWER(title) LIKE '%seller%'
                    THEN 1 ELSE 0
                END AS sales_title,
                CASE WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS sales_cycle,
            CASE WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS quota,
            CASE WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS pipeline,
            CASE WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS prospecting,
            CASE WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS revenue_target,
            CASE WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS closing,
            CASE WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS closing_alt,
            CASE WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS book_of_business,
            CASE WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS territory,
            CASE WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS commission,
            CASE WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS pre_sales,
            CASE WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS presales,
            CASE WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(description_plain,'')) LIKE ? THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS account_expansion
            FROM roles
            WHERE present_in_api = 1
        )
        SELECT *,
               (
                   sales_team + sales_title + sales_cycle + quota + pipeline + prospecting + revenue_target + closing + closing_alt + book_of_business + territory + commission + pre_sales + presales + account_expansion
               ) AS sales_signal_count
        FROM sales_signals
        WHERE (
            sales_team + sales_title + sales_cycle + quota + pipeline + prospecting + revenue_target + closing + closing_alt + book_of_business + territory + commission + pre_sales + presales + account_expansion
        ) > 0
        ORDER BY sales_signal_count DESC, team, title
```

### relevant_family_compensation

```sql
SELECT
                r.ashby_id,
                r.title,
                r.team,
                r.display_location,
                r.workplace_type,
                c.compensation_type,
                c.interval,
                c.currency_code,
                c.min_value,
                c.max_value
            FROM roles r
            JOIN api_compensation_components c
              ON c.ashby_id=r.ashby_id
            WHERE r.present_in_api=1
              AND r.team IN (
                    'Support Delivery',
                    'Support Operations',
                    'Support Automation',
                    'IT',
                    'Security',
                    'Trust & Safety',
                    'Intelligence & Investigations',
                    'Technical Success',
                    'User Operations',
                    'Technical Program Management'
                  )
            ORDER BY r.team, r.title, c.component_index
```

### data_quality_summary

```sql
SELECT
                COUNT(*) AS current_roles,
                SUM(CASE WHEN description_plain IS NULL
                              OR TRIM(description_plain)=''
                         THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS missing_description,
                SUM(CASE WHEN workplace_type IS NULL
                         THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS missing_workplace_type,
                SUM(CASE WHEN is_remote IS NULL
                         THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS missing_is_remote,
                SUM(CASE WHEN display_location IS NULL
                              OR TRIM(display_location)=''
                         THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS missing_primary_location,
                SUM(CASE WHEN published_at IS NULL
                         THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS missing_published_at
            FROM roles
            WHERE present_in_api=1
```

## 20. Interpretation notes established so far
- `is_remote=1` cannot be treated as equivalent to a fully remote job; many records pair it with `Hybrid`.
- Primary location alone is incomplete because the API contains secondary locations, including `US - Remote` on some roles whose primary location is a city.
- `US - Remote` plus `Hybrid` is ambiguous and requires description review rather than automatic Utah eligibility.
- Relocation assistance and permission to start remotely are separate questions.
- Sales exclusion cannot be performed by Sales-team membership alone; sales-adjacent responsibilities can occur in other teams.
- Technical relevance cannot be inferred from team/title alone; the actual work in the description matters.
- Keyword overlap with the resume is evidence-discovery only. It does not establish competence, seniority, or qualification.
- Experience and education language must eventually be separated into required vs preferred vs equivalent-experience statements before being used as hard gates.
- Compensation is useful as a seniority clue only when currency and interval are preserved.
